RP_realizing_mittelafrika = {
	RP_declare_mittelafrika = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_orginization_of_african_unity

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
		}

		available = {
			count_triggers = {
				amount = 10
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_ivorycoast
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_kenyauganda_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_centralafrica_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_katanga_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_westcentralafrica_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_sudwestafrica_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_sudcentralafrica_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_rhodesia_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_sudostafrica_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_njassaland_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_madagascar_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_mittelcongo_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_chad_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_kamerun_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_nigeria_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_togoland_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_ghana_decision
				has_country_flag = RP_dismantle_ivorycoast_decision
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		complete_effect = {
			KOA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = KOA_mittelafrika
				load_focus_tree = mittelafrikan_focus
			}
		}
	}

	RP_dismantle_kenyauganda = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_orginization_of_african_unity

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = RP_dismantle_colony_trigger_tt

				KKU = {
					any_neighbor_country = {
						tag = KOA
					}
				}
			}

			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = RP_dismantle_colony_trigger_tt.2

				NOT = { has_country_flag = RP_dismantling_colony }
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 30
		cost = 25

		visible = {
			country_exists = KKU
		}

		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = RP_dismantling_colony
		}

		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = RP_dismantle_colony_effect_tt
			clr_country_flag = RP_dismantling_colony

			KOA = {
				hidden_effect = {
					every_state = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_by = KKU
						}
						add_core_of = KOA
					}
					annex_country = {
						target = KKU
						transfer_troops = yes
					}
				}
				add_stability = -0.01
			}
			set_country_flag = RP_dismantle_kenyauganda_decision
		}
	}

	RP_establish_little_venice = {

		icon = GFX_decisions_RP_decision

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 40
		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 489
				state = 307
				state = 488
			}
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = RP_decisionrequirement_tt
				489 = { state_is_fully_controlled_by_ROOT_or_subject = yes }
				307 = { state_is_fully_controlled_by_ROOT_or_subject = yes }
				488 = { state_is_fully_controlled_by_ROOT_or_subject = yes }
			}
		} 

		visible = { 
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = RP_nofocus_imperial_puppets }
				has_completed_focus = GER_weltpolitik
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = RP.formation_tooltip.1

			hidden_effect = {
				KKV = {
					every_state = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								state = 489
								state = 307
								state = 488
							}
						}
						add_to_temp_array = {
							array = global.RP_puppetstates
							value = THIS.id
						}
					}

					RP_establish_colony = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = RP_establish_little_venice_decision
			}
		}
	}
